- **code-ops-suite-doc-alignment**: Use when docs have drifted from code and you want them reconciled into a clean single source of truth. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/tree/HEAD/opencode-dist/skills/code-ops-suite-doc-alignment))
- **code-ops-suite-everything**: Use when you want the most exhaustive end-to-end pass across all three plugins. It is token-expensive and checkpointed. It is the cross-plugin superset, and requires code-ops-suite, rigor, and privacy-opsec-suite installed. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/tree/HEAD/opencode-dist/skills/code-ops-suite-everything))
- **code-ops-suite-feature-discovery**: Use when you want grounded, high-value feature ideas mined from the codebase rather than a generic wishlist. Discovery only, and it writes no code. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/tree/HEAD/opencode-dist/skills/code-ops-suite-feature-discovery))
- **code-ops-suite-feature-implementation**: Use when feature specs already exist and you want them built incrementally. It requires specs as input. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/tree/HEAD/opencode-dist/skills/code-ops-suite-feature-implementation))
- **code-ops-suite-full-sweep**: Use when you want the whole code-ops-suite run end-to-end on one codebase as a guided, checkpointed pipeline. It is the intra-plugin orchestrator. For the cross-plugin superset use everything. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/tree/HEAD/opencode-dist/skills/code-ops-suite-full-sweep))
- **code-ops-suite-handoff**: Use when a long run must survive a context limit, session end, or operator change. It captures the run's true state as a verifiable HANDOFF.md, or resumes from one after re-verifying every claim. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/tree/HEAD/opencode-dist/skills/code-ops-suite-handoff))
